The historical script includes release notes in this tag.\n\nRestored four deleted branch refs and five deleted tag refs. The repository now has five branches, eight tags, and the five original remote-tracking refs. Develop and hotfix tips come from surviving local reflogs. The retry-policy tip is retained by the mirror ref and surviving history; the cache-warmup archive records the newer local tip. Release commits and their historical release scripts distinguish lightweight tags from annotated tags. Surviving annotated tags supplied their original object IDs.\n\nAll original ref files, packed refs, reflogs, HEAD, and configuration are byte-for-byte unchanged. No commit history was rewritten. No unrelated refs were created.\n\n## Final refs\n\n\"'```text\n{refs}```\n\nThe original damaged repository is preserved at `/app/recovery-work/original.git`. Recovery scripts and diagnostics are in `/app/recovery-work`.
